## Cinematic Beach Portrait at Golden Hour

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A soft, cinematic portrait of a young woman sitting on a driftwood log at a serene beach during golden hour. She wears a flowing white vintage-style dress with delicate lace details. Her hair is loosely tied back, slightly messy from the sea breeze. She looks back over her shoulder toward the camera with a calm, natural expression. Warm sunlight illuminates her face and dress, creating soft shadows and a dreamy glow. In the background, gentle ocean waves meet the sandy shore, with rocky cliffs adding texture and depth. Natural colors, high detail, shallow depth of field, realistic skin texture, 85mm lens, photorealistic, editorial fashion style.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Streetwear Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"Ultra-realistic full-body portrait of the same young man, maintaining strict facial identity and consistency with the reference image. He is positioned outdoors in a modern urban environment, with a softly blurred background featuring lush green plants and contemporary glass architecture.
The subject is captured in a natural walking pose — body slightly angled, one hand relaxed by his side while the other moves subtly forward. His head is gently turned to the left, creating a candid, editorial-style moment.
Wardrobe styling is minimal yet fashionable: an oversized black t-shirt with a small chest logo, black cargo pants, black sunglasses, a sleek crossbody sling bag, a wristwatch, and a subtle chain pendant — all styled in a modern streetwear aesthetic.
Lighting is soft natural daylight with cinematic quality, producing gentle highlights and realistic shadows across the subject. The atmosphere feels premium and lifestyle-focused.
Shot using an 85mm lens with a shallow depth of field — the subject remains in razor-sharp focus while the background falls into a smooth, creamy bokeh.
Textures are highly detailed and lifelike — including fabric folds, skin tones, accessories, and environmental elements.
Color grading is clean and modern with subtle contrast, enhancing realism while maintaining a polished cinematic tone.
8K resolution, ultra-sharp details, photorealistic, professional fashion editorial style, high dynamic range, premium composition."
```

## Hyper-Realistic Fashion Portrait with Reference Image

> Fotogerçekçi moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance, maintaining strong resemblance to the person in the reference. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 3:4.
Shot on a Phase One XF with a Schneider-Kreuznach 80mm f/2.8 LS lens, this hyper-realistic three-quarter length photograph captures an adult woman in a high-fashion studio setting, framed slightly off-center for a natural, candid feel. The subject exhibits a slender and athletic build with a sophisticated weight shift onto her right leg, causing her right hip to rise subtly while her torso is angled 15 degrees to the left. Her right hand rests gracefully on her upper thigh with fingers relaxed and softly curved, while her left arm is bent at a sharp angle with the hand placed higher on the hip, creating a dynamic, non-symmetrical silhouette. Her head is tilted slightly toward her right shoulder, maintaining a captivating and charismatic direct gaze. Her skin is depicted with microscopic detail, showcasing a radiant glass-skin finish with visible pores and a healthy glow on her exposed shoulders and collarbone. Her voluminous dark brown hair is styled in dramatic, cascading curls swept heavily to one side, creating an asymmetrical flow of texture and deep shadows. She wears an architectural couture gown made of shimmering, pleated icy-blue metallic lamé that catches the light along every sharp ridge. The bodice features intricate diagonal pleating and a structured sweetheart neckline, while a massive, fan-like sculptural ruffle erupts from the left shoulder. The skirt includes a wrap-around front with a high thigh-level slit, bordered by vertical cascading ruffles that suggest mid-motion fluidity. Her face is styled with a glamorous evening makeup look consisting of a soft mauve and charcoal smoky eye, sharp winged eyeliner, and defined dark brown brows, finished with a matte taupe lip color. She is adorned with large, tiered drop earrings in a matching matte blue finish. The lighting is professionally executed with a primary softbox from the front-side creating gentle shadows under the jawline and deep, cinematic contrast against a minimalist, dark charcoal grey studio backdrop with a smooth, non-reflective floor. Aspect Ratio 3:4.
```

## Moody East Asian Fashion Portrait in Bedroom

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Medium close-up shot from a high angle looking down, professional fashion photography of a beautiful young adult East Asian woman with fair skin, long soft black hair, and dark brown almond-shaped eyes. She is reclining on a bed in a three-quarter view pose, head slightly tilted, her left hand gently touching her right cheek. She has a calm, pensive expression, with a soft, distant gaze looking away from the camera. She wears an elegant black form-fitting top with a deep V-neckline, made of intricate floral lace with eyelash trim and scattered glossy sequins, and a single ornate gold earring in her left ear. The setting is a softly lit bedroom at night, against a dark background and a white fluffy comforter. The scene is illuminated by a single, soft, diffused key light from the front-left, casting subtle shadows on the right side of her face and body. Intimate, serene, and moody atmosphere. Photorealistic 8K, ultra detailed, best quality, sharp focus on face, shallow depth of field with soft background blur (bokeh). Shot on Sony A7R IV with an 85mm f/2.2 lens. natural pose, no exaggeration, stick to visible elements only.

**Recommended Parameters:**
--ar 3:4
--style raw

**Negative Prompt:**
blurry, harsh lighting, distorted features, bad anatomy, unnatural pose, watermark, text, oversaturated colors, looking directly at camera
```

## Drake with Orbiting Glass Music Interfaces

> Sinematik, minimal arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Numerous 3D Spotify and Apple Music interface cards orbiting Drake at varying depths.
Material: Translucent frosted glass (glassmorphism) with soft rounded edges and subtle glowing borders.
 Movement: Some cards are in the extreme foreground, partially obscuring Drake, while others drift into the distance behind him.
Visual Accents: Dynamic motion blur on the edges of the cards to suggest a swirling, orbiting movement.
LIGHTING & COMPOSITION
Lighting: Natural tones with enhanced professional photo quality. The soft glow from the interface cards subtly illuminates Drake’s face.
Focus: Strong depth of field. The background and extreme foreground cards are artistically blurred, keeping the focus sharp on Drake and the closest player interfaces.
Composition: A cinematic wide or medium shot that emphasizes the scale of the floating AR elements.
AESTHETIC
High-end editorial music photography, futuristic AR concept, clean and airy vibe. 8k resolution, sophisticated lighting, and realistic textures on the glass cards.
```

```
{
```

## Karina (aespa) in Ivory and Purple Hair

> Minimal, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
In a vertical composition of elegant proportions, Karina from aespa is seated upon a soft, grey-carpeted floor, her figure captured in a moment of poised, modern grace. She possesses a lithe and delicately curvaceous build, her skin radiating a luminous, porcelain fairness that speaks of her Korean heritage, smooth and ethereal against the muted backdrop. Her presence is one of striking aesthetic harmony, characterized by her sharp, feline-like facial features, refined symmetry, and a sophisticated, supermodel-like allure. Long, silken tresses of vibrant deep purple flow downward, veiling her shoulders and reaching toward the floor in a vivid cascade of color. She holds a dark smartphone before her face, her slender fingers adorned with a simple ring and pale, manicured nails, creating a focal point of contemporary mystery. She is dressed in a complex, ivory-white ensemble that blends delicate textures; a high-collared, sleeveless bodice features a circular cutout at the chest and draped, voluminous sleeves that gather at the wrists. The garment is cinched at the waist with intricate lace-up detailing, flowing into a sheer, skirt-like overlay that rests against her legs. Complementing the attire are white lace garters cinched around her thighs, adding a layer of intricate detail to the soft contours of her physique. The setting is minimalist and serene, defined by the neutral, textured carpet below and a backdrop of heavy, white draped fabric that falls in soft, vertical folds. The lighting is diffused and even, casting gentle shadows that define the subtle musculature of her arms and the graceful curve of her pose, bathing the entire scene in a clean, high-fashion glow that emphasizes the vivid violet of her hair against the pristine whites of her surroundings. —ar 9:16
```

```
using the attached reference photos as the identity and wardrobe source for the man, preserve the exact same facial features, face geometry, hairstyle and clothing from the references, create a photorealistic 2x2 collage of four cinematic travel lifestyle scenes with consistent color grading and atmosphere, as if shot on the same camera during one road trip. the same car model appears in all frames, matching color and interior. frame 1: man stepping out of the car on a mountain overlook at sunset, one hand on the open door, wind in hair, looking into the distance over clouds. frame 2: man sitting in the open trunk of the same car, playing an acoustic guitar, relaxed posture, warm autumn light, cozy blankets inside trunk. frame 3: interior car view from back seat, man driving, eyes visible in rearview mirror, one hand adjusting mirror, road glowing in golden light ahead. frame 4: man standing on a forest trail with backpack, looking up at tall trees, soft mist and green tones. natural cinematic lighting, cohesive warm earthy palette, subtle film grain, shallow depth of field, realistic skin texture with minimal professional retouching, lifestyle photography, 50mm lens look, f/2.8, no text, no watermark
```

```
A high-resolution, cinematic portrait of a stylish man with a well-groomed, thick beard and messy, textured dark hair. He is wearing classic black-lensed Ray-Ban aviator sunglasses with silver frames, which show subtle outdoor reflections. He is dressed in a black pebbled-leather jacket with the collar popped, layered over a crisp white t-shirt and a thin silver chain necklace with a small circular pendant. The man is leaning out of a dark, high-end vehicle, creating a sense of motion or a brief pause in a journey. The lighting is bright and natural, casting soft highlights on his forehead and the texture of the leather. The background is a soft-focus, creamy bokeh of lush green trees and foliage, emphasizing a shallow depth of field. The overall mood is cool, masculine, and sophisticated, with a neutral but punchy color palette.
```

## Fashion Editorial Portrait in Modern Bathroom

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high-resolution, ultra-realistic portrait with precise facial resemblance (face match accuracy prioritized).
A stylish young man is posing confidently in a warm-toned, minimalistic bathroom interior. The environment features soft ambient lighting, neutral textures, and a modern circular mirror that subtly frames the subject.
He is wearing an oversized bold red T-shirt with a trendy “LV” graphic print, paired with dark casual pants. He holds a white New York (NY) cap casually in one hand and wears a sleek black wristwatch.
His expression is relaxed and self-assured, conveying a cool, effortless aesthetic. Lighting should be soft and flattering, with warm highlights and gentle shadows to enhance depth and skin tone.
Emphasize sharp details, natural skin texture, balanced contrast, and a clean, premium fashion-editorial look. Depth of field should be subtle, keeping the subject crisp while slightly softening the background.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Tropical Portrait with Smartphone Aesthetic

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance, maintaining strong resemblance to the person in the reference. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 4:5.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, characterized by its digital clarity, captures a woman in a vibrant outdoor tropical setting under bright sunlight. She is positioned with her left hand gently touching her long, straight, dark brown hair, which is parted down the middle, while her right hand rests on her hip, and her gaze is directed downwards. She is dressed in a white deep V-neck swimsuit or bodysuit with subtle side cutouts, paired with light wash denim jeans and a flowing, sheer pink floral kimono featuring bell sleeves and vibrant yellow, blue, and green floral patterns. She accessorizes with delicate layered necklaces, one bearing a hamsa charm, and simple hoop earrings. Her makeup is subtle, featuring a soft winged eyeliner and natural pink lips. The background is in full, sharp focus without any bokeh or background blur, revealing lush green tropical foliage, a prominent tree with detailed branches, and a textured bamboo fence, all bathed in intense sunlight with prominent sun lens flares. This image, captured on an iPhone 15 Pro, exhibits a realistic, digital texture with ultra-sharp details, including naturally pinkish, dewy skin with a Korean glass skin finish and visible pores, completely free of film grain but with a hint of subtle digital noise. The lighting is bright and exceptionally even, typical of modern smartphone HDR processing, ensuring that shadows are lifted and never appear deep black, presenting a natural and true-to-life color palette.
```

## Minimalist Fashion Studio Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a photorealistic minimalist fashion studio portrait of a woman.
The face should be natural and realistic, with well-balanced proportions and subtle skin texture. Avoid over-beautification or artificial features — maintain a believable, editorial look.
Pose:
The woman is seated in a relaxed editorial pose on a simple geometric prop (such as a cube or stool), body angled slightly toward the camera. One leg is casually crossed over the other. One arm rests naturally on the knee while the other hand is placed near the face or relaxed on the lap. Calm, confident, slightly introspective expression, looking toward the camera.
Outfit:
A modern, stylish outfit — for example: an oversized {argument name="blazer color" default="beige"} blazer layered over a white fitted tank top, high-waisted tailored trousers, and minimal leather sandals or heels. Natural fabric flow and realistic textures should be visible.
Environment:
Background: Soft neutral seamless backdrop (light gray, warm beige, or subtle gradient).
Props: Minimal and clean, matching the aesthetic (simple cube or stool only).
Lighting:
Soft diffused studio lighting with gentle shadows, natural skin tones, smooth highlight falloff, and subtle depth. No harsh contrasts.
Composition:
Full-body or 3/4 editorial framing, centered subject, strong negative space, clean minimalist composition.
Style:
Modern fashion editorial
Minimal, elegant, contemporary
Neutral and cohesive color palette
Subtle film grain
Realistic fabric folds and textures
```

```
Create a highly realistic cinematic portrait of a cool young man based on the uploaded reference photo, preserving the same facial identity, facial structure, and overall look. The young man has wavy vintage style hair, neatly styled with a clean cut appearance that frames his face naturally. His expression is calm and confident as he looks slightly to the right, giving a relaxed and thoughtful vibe.

He is seated comfortably on a simple wooden crate in a studio setting. His posture is casual with one leg slightly forward and his shoulders relaxed. In one hand he loosely holds a denim jacket draped naturally over his arm as if he just took it off.

He is wearing a plain loose white t shirt with a soft cotton texture and light colored baggie jeans that fit casually. The fabric folds and natural creases add realism to the clothing while maintaining a minimal stylish look.

The background is a dramatic black studio environment with atmospheric smoke effects in {argument name="smoke color 1" default="blue"} and {argument name="smoke color 2" default="red"} tones slowly spreading behind him. The colored smoke creates a soft glowing contrast against the dark background and adds depth to the scene. Subtle studio lighting highlights the edges of his hair, shoulders, and clothing, while soft shadows fall around the wooden crate.

Professional studio portrait photography style, captured with a full frame camera using an 85mm lens. Shallow depth of field keeps the subject in sharp focus while the colored smoke softly blurs in the background. Ultra photorealistic lighting, natural skin texture, cinematic color grading, high contrast, extremely detailed, 8k resolution.
```

## 9-Panel Editorial Grid Prompt for Yellow Satin Halter Dress

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "yellow_satin_halter_flower_grid",
      "language": "en",
      "priority": "highest",
      "version": "v1.1_YELLOW_TULIP_9GRID_SATIN_HALTER_NO_SAME"
    },
    "input": {
      "mode": "text_to_image",
      "notes": "Create a photoreal 3x3 editorial portrait grid featuring one elegant adult woman across all nine panels. She is holding a large bouquet of fresh yellow tulips in every frame. Her outfit is a refined butter-yellow satin halter dress tied at the neck, softly body-skimming and feminine, with a luxurious glossy fabric finish. She has long soft wavy hair, polished and romantic. Each panel must show a different pose and expression while preserving clear facial consistency, bouquet consistency, soft indoor setting, and the warm minimal editorial mood. The poses should feel natural, graceful, and varied: gentle smile, soft side glance, looking down at the flowers, subtle head tilt, bouquet held higher, bouquet cradled lower, one hand adjusting hair, relaxed three-quarter pose, and elegant front-facing pose."
    },
    "output_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "4:5",
      "orientation": "portrait",
      "resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
      "num_images": 1,
      "layout": {
        "type": "grid",
        "rows": 3,
        "cols": 3,
        "gutter": "thin",
        "outer_border": "none",
        "panel_consistency": "very_high"
      },
      "render_style": "soft_luxury_editorial_realism",
      "sharpness": "natural_crisp",
      "grain": "subtle_film",
      "dynamic_range": "soft_rich_natural",
      "color_grade": "warm_creamy_daylight"
    },
    "subject": {
      "identity": "one woman with highly consistent facial features across all nine panels",
      "hair": "long soft wavy dark hair",
      "outfit": "{argument name="outfit description" default="butter-yellow satin halter dress tied at the neck, elegant and softly fitted"}",
      "accessories": "minimal delicate jewelry"
    },
    "scene": {
      "environment": "minimal indoor studio-like wall background with soft warm daylight",
      "props": "{argument name="prop" default="large bouquet of yellow tulips"}",
      "mood": "romantic, polished, feminine, soft, elegant"
    },
    "camera": {
      "framing": "mixed medium and three-quarter fashion portrait shots",
      "angle": "soft editorial portrait angles",
      "focus": "sharp on face, tulips, and satin texture",
      "depth_of_field": "clean soft separation"
    },
    "lighting": {
      "type": "soft natural indoor light",
      "quality": "warm flattering light with delicate shadows",
      "skin_rendering": "realistic skin texture, no plastic effect"
    },
    "must_have": [
      "3x3 grid layout",
      "one woman repeated across all panels with strong identity consistency",
      "yellow satin halter dress",
      "long wavy hair",
      "large yello"
    ]
  }
}
```

```
A cinematic vertical fashion editorial portrait (9:16) of a beautiful woman with long, airy black hair, softly wavy and floating weightlessly as if suspended in a gentle breeze.
She wears an ethereal haute couture gown in soft ivory, pale champagne, and luminous pearl tones. The fabric is light and translucent layered chiffon, silk, and organza that float and diffuse light beautifully. 

Her face is softly lit in full clarity — glowing skin, delicate features, serene expression, soft radiant makeup with a dewy finish.
She gently holds a transparent crystal sphere near her heart.
Inside the sphere: a luminous miniature universe — pastel nebula clouds, glowing stardust, soft galaxies, dreamy gradients, and faint distant lights. The internal world is soft and magical with gentle refraction.

 A subtle inverted silhouette of her appears within, blending harmoniously with the glowing cosmos.
Background: soft cosmic gradient in pale blues, lavender, and silver tones, filled with diffused glowing stars and dreamy haze.
Lighting: soft diffused lighting, glowing highlights, gentle bloom effect, subtle backlight creating a halo-like aura.
Ultra-realistic, soft contrast, dreamy cinematic depth, 8k resolution.
Mood: ethereal, soft, celestial, poetic, dreamlike elegance.
```

```
プロンプト2（程よい版本）：画像を忠実な構成と色を保ちながら強化・アップスケールします。ぼかしを取り除き、定義された毛孔と影とハイライト間の自然な移行を含むリアルな肌詳細を実現します。明るいトーンと背景を維持し、目、毛睫毛、唇、髪の周囲の輪郭のシャープさを調整し、ポートレートにプレミアムビューティー写真の自然に加工されていない肌質感を与えます。
```

## Black and White Cinematic Motorcycle Portrait

> Sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-contrast, black-and-white cinematic portrait of a rugged man in the uploaded image wearing sunglasses with a well-groomed beard and messy, voluminous hair. He is leaning casually against a classic-style modern motorcycle (resembling a BMW R NineT), with his left arm resting atop a black open-face helmet placed on the bike's seat. He wears a dark, short-sleeved button-down shirt over a t-shirt and light-colored denim jeans. The man is looking off-camera to the left with a contemplative expression. The motorcycle features intricate details, including spoked wheels, a dual exhaust system, and a metallic fuel tank with pinstripe detailing. The background is a beautifully blurred (shallow depth of field) architectural setting featuring stone arches and weathered masonry, suggesting an old-world or European street. The lighting is soft and directional, coming from the side to accentuate the textures of the denim, the metallic glint of the bike, and the contours of the man's face and forearms. Fine film grain and high tonal range from deep blacks to crisp whites.
```

```
4.Stylish pose running hand through hair while holding blazer.

Realism: natural skin pores, hair strands, subtle fabric wrinkles, realistic shadows, lens compression, reflections in sunglasses.
Output: ultra-realistic GQ/Vogue-style editorial photography, sharp detail, 4K resolution, clean 2×2 grid layout.
```

## Ultra-realistic Full-Body Fashion Studio Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
proportions, hairline, and overall likeness with high accuracy. Do not beautify, stylize, or alter their identity.

Facial expression:
calm, confident, or warm smile with direct eye contact toward the camera.

Body Proportions

Maintain realistic human proportions based on the person reference image.

Preserve:

body shape

shoulder width

natural posture

Do not exaggerate body shape.

Outfit Recreation

The subject must wear the exact outfit from the outfit reference image.

Recreate clothing accurately including:

colors

fabric type

patterns and prints

garment structure

folds and stitching

clothing layers

The outfit should appear naturally worn and properly fitted to the subject.

Pose Logic (Important)

Do not copy the pose from any reference image.

Instead, determine the pose based on the style of the outfit.

Examples:

Modern / streetwear outfits → relaxed modern fashion pose
Formal outfits → confident professional standing pose
Casual outfits → natural relaxed pose

The pose should look like a professional fashion studio photoshoot.

Composition

Professional studio fashion portrait.

Full body framing from head to toe (very important).

Aspect ratio 4:5 vertical.

Subject positioned naturally in the center of the frame.

Background

Create a clean professional studio sweep background.

Background characteristics:

smooth light grey seamless backdrop

backdrop curves smoothly into the floor

darker grey studio floor

subtle gradient lighting on the background

No objects or props.

Lighting

Professional studio lighting:

soft front key light

balanced fill light

subtle rim light for separation

evenly illuminated background

Lighting must look natural and professional.

Image Style

Ultra-realistic studio photography.

Requirements:

photorealistic skin texture

detailed fabric rendering

sharp focus

natural shadows

professional fashion photography look

Restrictions

No extra people
No text
No logos
No props
No background objects
No half-body portrait
Subject must be fully visible from head to toe
```

```
Typography: Bold, white, geometric sans-serif text reading "{argument name=\"main text\" default=\"Bold Impact\"}" is overlaid on the lower-right cheek. Three smaller, delicate labels are scattered: "Deep Tones" (top right), "Sharp Detail" (on the left lens), and "Strong Style" (bottom right).
Color Palette: A rich mix of skin tones (#E8A88A), burnt orange (#B8541C), and deep espresso (#1A0D0B), contrasted with crisp white text (#FFFFFF).
Lighting: Warm studio lighting with a soft-focus depth of field. The background consists of out-of-focus hair and skin, keeping all attention on the eye and the texture of the acetate frames.
Atmosphere: Premium, editorial fashion magazine aesthetic; high-contrast but with soft transitions.
```

The lighting creates soft shadows and a calm atmosphere.
```
Shot in a clean editorial lifestyle photography style, shallow depth of field, natural skin tones, ultra-detailed textures in the linen fabric, sharp focus on the subject, soft background blur, balanced composition, 85mm lens look, high resolution, photorealistic, modern luxury aesthetic, warm evening ambiance.
```

## Photorealistic Moodboard Generation Prompt (Nano Banana 2)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Masterpiece, best quality, (photorealistic:1.6), (moodboard collage:1.7), editorial fashion layout, magazine spread.

(Central Focus - CRITICAL KEEP):
In the center of the composition, the (person from the input image is placed EXACTLY as they are:1.8), maintaining their pose, clothing, expression, and photorealistic style.
They are the anchor of the collage.

(Surrounding Elements - Inference & Collage):
Surrounding the central person are overlapping, layered elements inferred from their style, color palette, and vibe.
(Color Palette): Several (color swatches with hex codes:1.5) reflecting the outfit's main colors (e.g., muted tones, vibrant accents).
(Textures): Circular or square cutouts showing close-up (fabric texture samples:1.6) of the clothes they are wearing (e.g., denim, silk, leather, knitwear).
(Fashion Items): Polaroid-style photos or cutouts of matching (accessories and shoes:1.5) (e.g., handbags, jewelry, sneakers, boots) arranged around them.
(Text & Vibe): Torn paper snippets with elegant typography showing style keywords like "EFFORTLESS", "URBAN CHIC", "MINIMALIST", or "VINTAGE VIBES".

(Layout & Aesthetic):
The elements are arranged in a stylish, slightly messy (scrapbook collage style:1.5) with torn paper edges, masking tape, and paper clips.
The background of the board is a textured paper or cork board.
High-end fashion magazine aesthetic.

(Technical Tags): (collage art:1.5), (fashion scrapbook), (material board), (layered), (aesthetic layout), sharp focus on the central person.
(Negative prompt): (illustration:1.8), (painting:1.8), (3d render:1.8), (anime style:1.8), (changing person's face:2.0), (changing clothing:2.0), (cartoon), (low quality), (blurry textures), (messy layout:1.5).
```

She is in a low, crouched squat position on the floor, balancing on the spiked high heels and both hands are placed flat on her thighs, leaning slightly forward, and looking directly at the camera. Her accessories include a ruby bead necklace with a gold star pendant, silver hoop earrings, gold rings, and a bright pink bracelet with a raised texture. Aspect ratio 4:5."

## Minimalist Side-Profile Portrait for Character Consistency

> Minimal, stüdyo ışıklı moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
MAIN SUBJECT (IDENTITY)

Use the person from the reference photo as the main subject. Keep their exact facial features, hairstyle, skin tone, and expression perfectly unchanged preserve all realism, emotional depth, and subtle facial lighting. Allow changes only in camera position, background motion, and atmosphere. A minimalist side-profile portrait of a new person, framed from the chest up and positioned in strict profile against a textured dark concrete wall (approx. #282F32). The pose remains relaxed and composed, with the subject facing left, eyes gently closed or lowered, maintaining a calm, introspective expression. Style the subject in monochromatic, winter-inspired streetwear: a black beanie, thin metal-frame glasses, and a high-collar or turtleneck layered under a heavy textured coat. Use soft, diffused natural lighting from above and slightly in front, with a cool temperature around 4800K, creating gentle shadows that sculpt the jawline and highlight the contours of the face without harsh contrast. Emphasize the velvety matte textures of the clothing and the subtle grain of the wall. Background remains static and uniform, allowing the subject's silhouette to stand out cleanly. Use a shallow depth of field with a medium portrait lens (50-85mm) to maintain crisp facial detail while softly separating the subject from the background. Color palette rooted in deep blacks, muted grays, and natural skin tones. Mood calm, intimate, and urban-modern, with a clean editorial aesthetic. Editorial, modern, high-detail, cinematic composition, ultra-realistic textures, professional fashion photography style
```

```
      "makeup": "soft glam beauty campaign makeup, sculpted skin, defined eyes, elegant lashes, nude-peach lips",
      "wardrobe": "sleek salmon-colored fitted two-piece fashion set",
      "accessories": "luxurious gold high heels",
      "expression": "confident, glamorous, calm"
```

```
Keep the garment realistic and undistorted no warping of logos or graphics. Maintain correct scale and print alignment.
Photorealistic. 8K resolution. Natural color grading. No added text. No watermark.
```

## Minimalist Cinematic Fashion Editorial Studio Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Using a person's photo as the identity reference, create a minimalist cinematic fashion-editorial studio portrait. Vertical 4:5, mid-torso to head framing with generous negative space above, subject slightly left of center.

Pose: shoulders angled 3/4, head turned back toward camera, chin slightly lifted. Expression calm, cool, slightly aloof, eyes relaxed and focused.

Wardrobe: {argument name="wardrobe" default="black smoking (tuxedo jacket)"}, no logos, no jewelry.

Lighting: dramatic high-contrast studio lighting. A strong neutral key light from camera-right/front-right sculpting the face and coat, deep falloff into shadow on the far side, with a very subtle low-intensity soft fill from camera-left.

Background: completely black studio environment. Behind the subject’s head, a soft diffused backlight creating a subtle glowing halo with cinematic bloom, not a solid circle. The light softly radiates outward and gradually dissolves into pure black (#000000), creating a gentle atmospheric glow around the silhouette. No visible light source, no hard edges, no textures.

Optics: 85mm portrait look, f/2–f/2.8 shallow depth of field (eyes tack sharp, background creamy), pronounced highlight bloom, soft halation around the backlight, smooth cinematic contrast, subtle film grain, crisp micro-texture in fabric weave, controlled natural skin texture.

No text, no watermark. Editorial, modern, high-detail, cinematic composition, ultra-realistic textures, professional fashion photography style.
```

## Noir Portrait with Balaclava and Cigarette Smoke

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A dark, mysterious noir style portrait. In the center is a young man wearing a tight white knitted ribbed balaclava mask, leaving only the eyes and mouth visible. The bright white mask contrasts strongly with the dark background. One intense male eye with subtle dark eyeliner looks directly at the camera. Through the lower opening, masculine lips release thick swirling cigarette smoke.

Dense smoke fills the space around his head, forming complex translucent patterns and appearing three dimensional under directional light. In his right hand he holds a lit cigarette between short natural nails, which is the source of the smoke.

Hard directional lighting from above or the side highlights the texture of the white mask and smoke while the shoulders and background stay in deep shadow. Monochrome palette of black, white, and gray with natural skin tones. The man wears a gray men's shirt with a soft matte texture, the collar and upper chest slightly visible in shadow. The atmosphere feels bold, mysterious, and urban.
```

## Hyper-Realistic iPhone Whale Breach Scene

```
Hyper-realistic, 8K DSLR quality, 4:5 aspect ratio, low-angle cinematic shot slightly tilted, 85mm lens, very shallow depth of field, razor sharp focus on the iPhone screen and the breaching whale inside it

Scene: Premium iPhone 17 Pro Max placed flat on a dark textured wooden table in an indoor studio, directional soft daylight from the left creating dramatic shadows and highlights, ultra-cinematic mood with intense dramatic tension before the peak of the breach

The smartphone screen magically transforms into a miniature ocean surface world strictly contained within the display borders:
deep blue ocean water filling the entire screen up to the surface, realistic rippling waves and small whitecaps on the water surface, no deep underwater view visible – only the surface and sky reflection at the top edge of the screen, subtle horizon line implied

A single majestic humpback whale frozen mid-breach right at the peak moment inside the screen:
relatively small scale compared to the phone size (miniature but powerfully detailed), body partially out of the water launching upward, flukes (tail) powerfully slapping the surface sending water exploding in dramatic arcs, pleated throat expanded, knobbly tubercles on head clearly visible, glossy wet black skin with water streaming off, intelligent eye catching light, water droplets and spray frozen in motion blur but all strictly contained within the screen edges – no overflow outside the display

Cinematic strong rim light and backlight dramatically outlining the whale against the ocean surface and sky, subtle lens flares from sunlight hitting wet skin and splashes, dynamic water explosion with realistic droplets catching the light, caustics faintly visible just below the surface ripple

Realism micro-details:
subtle realistic finger smudges and glossy reflections on the glass screen
tiny premium scratches on the titanium frame
floating dust motes in the air catching the directional light
screen's own glow softly reflecting off the whale's wet skin, splashing water droplets, and wave surfaces inside the miniature scene

Phone materials: brushed metallic premium frame with rich specular highlights and subtle color shifts
Table: dark rich wood with visible natural grain and faint wear marks

Mood: high-end luxury brand commercial meets epic nature moment, majestic power in miniature scale, awe-inspiring, intense cinematic premium aesthetic, sense of contained wild energy inside a sleek device

maximum photorealism, extreme micro texture detail on whale skin, water splashes, waves and caustics, cinema-grade lighting and sharpness, 8K hyper-detailed render
```

## Ultra-realistic Cinematic Scene of Internal Conflict

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic 8K cinematic scene of a young man is sitting at a wooden chess table in a dimly lit room, playing chess against himself. Two identical versions of him sit opposite each other, leaning slightly forward, elbows resting near the board, fingers gently touching chess pieces. They are locked eye-to-eye, intense and thoughtful, as if silently debating a life-altering move.
Left version: calm, composed expression, neutral body language, subtle warm side lighting.
Right version: slightly intense gaze, deeper shadows on face, dramatic low-key lighting.
A single overhead pendant light casts a focused beam on the chessboard, creating sharp shadows and a moody atmosphere. Background fades into darkness with soft cinematic haze. The chess pieces are mid-game, symbolizing internal conflict and strategic thinking.
Camera angle: medium close-up, eye-level perspective, shallow depth of field, sharp focus on both faces and the chessboard.
Color tone: dark cinematic blues and warm highlights.
Mood: psychological tension, introspection, duality, silent confrontation.
Hyper-detailed skin texture, realistic muscle definition (average athletic build, not exaggerated), natural posture, dramatic contrast lighting, editorial photography style.
```

```
create an image of a high-impact editorial studio portrait using the person from the attached reference photo as the only identity source preserve the subject’s facial structure, skin tone, proportions, hairstyle, age, and overall likeness exactly as in the reference image; do not modify, enhance, reshape, beautify, smooth, or reinterpret any facial features; enforce absolute identity accuracy frame a medium close-up (head and shoulders) from a low-angle perspective looking slightly upward to create a powerful, dominant presence pose the subject with shoulders squared and posture strong; direct the gaze off-camera and upward into space; hold a serious, focused, intense expression dress the subject in a premium matte-black pique polo shirt with visible cotton mesh texture; keep the fit sharp, tailored, and structured with a clean silhouette set a solid seamless studio backdrop with smooth, rich gradients only; include no patterns,no texture, and no props light with bold high-contrast studio lighting  use a strong directional key light to create dramatic chiaroscuro shadows emphasizing facial structure and jawline; add a powerful rim light/edge glow to separate head and shoulders from the background; maintain depth and dimensionality grade for a heroic, intense, commanding, cinematic editorial mood capture ultra-photorealistic detail with sharp focus on the face, visible natural skin texture and pores, no smoothing; keep background gradients smooth and clean shoot in high resolution with studio grade technical quality and a professional editorial finish
```

## Polychrome Carved Wooden Sculpture Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
SCULPTURE MATERIAL
The subject is carved from painted wood or resin (polychrome sculpture style).

Skin is no longer skin:
It becomes a matte carved surface with visible tool marks, tiny cracks, subtle paint wear, and hardened edges.

Add:
• chisel planes on cheeks and forehead
• carved eyelids and nostrils
• slightly flattened planes instead of soft flesh
• painted but slightly aged coloration

HAIR (CRITICAL — THIS DEFINES THE STYLE)
Hair must NOT look real.

The hair is sculpted:
• carved locks and grooves
• thick grouped strands
• simplified shapes
• visible chisel cuts
• rigid structure
• no flyaways, no softness, no realistic strands

Think: artisan statue hair, not human hair.

CLOTHING
Clothing becomes carved fabric:
• simplified folds
• hardened drapery
• painted texture
• slightly worn edges

COMPOSITION
• chest-up bust
• mounted on a round pedestal base
• centered framing
• neutral studio background
• soft museum lighting
• photographed like an art catalog

AVOID
real skin texture
photoreal pores
```

```
bone structure and jawline , eye shape and spacing
nose structure

hairstyle, hairline, and parting

shoulders relaxed and natural
head gently turned to the right

Expression: soft, distant, contemplative and thoughtful.

Hand Pose
Her right hand is raised thoughtfully toward her face:
wrist elegantly bent

index finger resting lightly against the lower lip
thumb placed beneath the chin area
remaining fingers softly curled inward
anatomically correct hand structure

visible knuckle definition
natural fingernails
delicate finger proportions

Hair

soft natural volume

loose strands around the temples and forehead
wispy strands framing the ear
one thin strand falling vertically across the left cheek

Clothing

She wears an elegant wrap-style V-neck blouse made from soft, flowing fabric resembling silk or satin.
Garment details include:
overlapping wrap front forming a deep V neckline
a fabric belt or tie cinching the blouse at the waist
soft draping folds radiating from the waist knot
fluid fabric tension across the torso
Sleeves:
long, slightly loose sleeves
gathered fabric volume along the forearm

deep natural folds at the inner elbow due to the raised arm pose
Cuffs:
structured cuff with a clean tailored edge
single round button fastening
vertical slit placket opening beside the button
```

## Photorealistic Lifestyle/Editorial Photo Shoot Prompt with Identity Preservation

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a photorealistic lifestyle/editorial photo shoot of the « casual flash in a street café » type, using a provided photo of a person as a reference. The person's appearance must be strictly identical to the model: perfectly recognizable face (100%), same facial proportions and geometry, same shape of eyes, nose, and lips, same complexion, same age, same hairstyle, and same hairline. Do not modify the face, do not « enhance » it, and do not change the gender. The setting is fixed: an evening café/lounge area (interior), beige walls, a large window overlooking the dark night, a wooden bar counter along the window, simple furniture: black metal chairs, wooden tables. To the left, a large potted indoor plant. No readable signs, logos, or text on the walls or windows. The composition is fixed: a vertical full-length shot, the camera at chest height, the person standing slightly to the right of center. In the foreground, on the left, a black metal chair is placed on a white backpack adorned with a black graphic print (no readable text). The background is slightly blurred, thus emphasizing the character. The lighting is fixed: a powerful flash (direct camera flash), marked shadows on the wall and floor, a « spam » photo style, like that of a compact camera or an iPhone. The attire is also fixed: an oversize white t-shirt without a logo or inscription, wide black pants, white sneakers with black stripes (retro style), a belt, a chain, or keys at the waist (unbranded) are acceptable. The pose is fixed: the person is standing, relaxed, next to the chair, the body slightly turned three-quarters; the right hand is raised and touches the head/hair (palm on the nape of the neck), the left hand is lowered near the hip. The legs: one leg is extended, the other slightly bent, the heel raised, creating a model posture. The head is turned to the side (left), the gaze is not fixed on the lens, the expression is calm and confident, without a smile. Quality/Style: ultra-realistic, sharp focus on the face, slight depth of field, natural complexion, light grain, no retouching, glossy appearance. Important: rigorously correct anatomy: two arms, two legs, five fingers, no deformities or supernumerary limbs.p
```

## Nano Banana Pro Product Assembly Video Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded image as the exact visual reference for jar design, label, strawberries, raspberries, flowers, leaves, condensation droplets, red gradient background, and reflective surface.

Camera:
Locked front-facing camera.
No zoom.
No rotation.
No angle change.
Studio composition remains centered.

Style:
Premium beauty commercial.
Smooth assembling sequence.
Ultra realistic.
Slow motion but not dramatic.

Scene – Start (Empty Setup):
Start with an empty red gradient background and glossy reflective surface.
No jar.
No fruits.
No flowers.
No water droplets.

Assembling Sequence:

1. The glass jar base smoothly slides into frame from below and settles perfectly centered.
2. The lid gently lowers from above and aligns perfectly on the jar.
3. Condensation droplets subtly appear on the glass surface as if forming naturally.
4. Raspberries roll in softly from left and right, stopping naturally around the base.
5. Strawberry halves slide into position at the front.
6. Whole strawberries gently drop from above into the open jar top and settle naturally.
7. Small white flowers softly fall into place between the strawberries.
8. Green leaves slide in behind the jar to frame it.
9. Final micro water droplets appear on fruits for freshness effect.

All movements smooth and controlled.
No bouncing.
No exaggerated motion.
No dramatic lighting changes.

Lighting:
Keep original studio lighting.
Deep red background glow remains consistent.
Soft reflections on the glossy surface.

Sound (if supported):
Soft subtle assembling sound design.
Gentle fruit placement sounds.
Light glass contact sound.
No music or very subtle premium ambient tone.

End Frame:
Fully assembled composition exactly matching the reference image.
Everything perfectly arranged.
Hold for 1–2 seconds.
Ultra clean luxury skincare commercial finish.
```

```
Do this for a famous Asian dish<instruction> Input A is a famous dish (photo or dish name). Deeply analyze its origin country or region, the historical or legendary figure most associated with its creation or popularization, traditional preparation methods, cultural significance, and signature sensory elements. Optional Input B is a style reference. If missing, fuse Michelin-star refinement with kaleidoscopic prism sculpture.
Goal: Premium crystal-clear acrylic box containing a mirrored kaleidoscope chamber where the dish scene repeats in infinite symmetrical patterns.
Rules:
Center: single inventor figurine preparing the dish at the focal point, reflected into a mesmerizing symmetrical diorama
Ingredient compartments arranged in geometric radial symmetry
Miniature tools duplicated in the mirrored reflections
Origin map fractured and repeated as a mandala backdrop
Box edges create rainbow refractions from flag-inspired tints
Bilingual label positioned so it multiplies beautifully in the reflections with origin date
Output: ONE image, 4:5 artisan food photography style
</instruction>
```

## Gritty Tom Cruise Portrait with GUCCI Branding

> Sinematik, minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A highly detailed, cinematic portrait of {argument name="subject name" default="Tom Cruise"}, featuring a gritty, industrial aesthetic. He has messy, spiked dark-blonde hair, facial stubble, and a look of intense, weary focus. He is captured in a close-up shot, holding a lit cigarette to his mouth with his right hand; a thick trail of white smoke curls upward. On his finger is a prominent silver signet ring engraved with a luxury interlocking "GG" logo. The man wears a distressed, dark red leather jacket heavily marked with white paint splatters and deep creases, layered over a filthy, stained cream-colored canvas apron. The center of the frame features the word "GUCCI" in a clean, white, authoritative serif font. Below it, in smaller text on the apron, is the phrase "IS AI STILL JUST A PLAYTHING?" followed by a second, faded "GUCCI" logo. The background is a blurred industrial laboratory or "soap factory" setting. To the right are stacks of translucent pink rectangular soap blocks. In the upper background, dark industrial machinery and hanging wires are illuminated by glowing red horizontal neon lights, creating a moody, high-contrast atmosphere. The entire image has a heavy film grain, a hazy atmosphere, and a color palette dominated by deep reds, pale pinks, and grimy earth tones.
```

```
"Create a high-quality realistic vertical fashion photo using the exact face from the reference (no changes). Cinematic full-length shot from a slightly low camera angle with shallow depth of field.

2. Side-sit pose: Sitting sideways on the seat with legs outside the car, one hand touching hair, looking toward camera.

3. Forward lean pose: Sitting upright among flowers, elbows resting on knees, slightly leaning toward camera.
```

```
Frame 2 (middle): medium shot from behind or from the side. The person is looking at the sea. Their coat and hair are moving in the wind. Seagulls fly nearby.
Frame 3 (bottom): close-up emotional portrait. Wind in the hair, natural skin texture, thoughtful gaze, cinematic mood.
Style: real photography, cinematic lifestyle portrait, documentary fashion photography.

Camera: full-frame DSLR look, 85mm lens aesthetic, shallow depth of field.
```

```
A hyper-realistic surreal composite scene set on a wooden tabletop. A large oversized smartphone dominates the frame, creating a strong dimensional-break illusion. ## Gourmet Collectible Cinematic Dish Visualization
```

> Sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
<instruction> Input A is a famous dish. Study its origin, credited creator, preparation ritual, cultural importance, and core ingredients. Optional Input B is style reference. Default: cinematic still meets gourmet collectible. Goal: Transparent acrylic cube staged as a single frozen frame from an imagined biographical film about the dish’s creation. Center: the inventor dramatically preparing the dish under theatrical miniature lighting, mid-gesture, with steam or flame effects. Surrounding compartments contain ingredients arranged as film props (close-ups of herbs, spices, vessels). Background subtly recreates the historical kitchen or street setting with tiny architectural details made from food. Include a tiny clapperboard or film reel element showing the dish name and origin year. Color palette drawn from classic cinema posters of the origin country. Bilingual title in period-appropriate font. Output: ONE image, 16:9 horizontal, moody cinematic lighting with high contrast and subtle lens flare. </instruction>
```
